========== USER ==========
xem giùm tôi khi kết nối tới host là windows 11 và chạy 1 app cần quyền administrator thì hiện hộp thoại này và treo phím chuột luôn

========== MODEL ==========
Đây là vấn đề kinh điển với **UAC (User Account Control) Secure Desktop** trên Windows. Để tôi xem code của bạn trước để hiểu cách host đang capture screen và xử lý input.

39: # Host Utilities
40: def encrypt_text(text, key="AntigravityP2P"):
41:     if not text:
42:         return ""
43:     xored = []
44:     for i, c in enumerate(text):
45:         xored_char = ord(c) ^ ord(key[i % len(key)])
46:         xored.append(chr(xored_char))
47:     import base64
48:     return base64.b64encode("".join(xored).encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')
49: 
50: def decrypt_text(encrypted_text, key="AntigravityP2P"):
51:     if not encrypted_text:
52:         return ""
53:     import base64
54:     try:
55:         decoded = base64.b64decode(encrypted_text.encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')
56:         xored = []
57:         for i, c in enumerate(decoded):
58:             xored_char = ord(c) ^ ord(key[i % len(key)])
59:             xored.append(chr(xored_char))
60:         return "".join(xored)
61:     except Exception:
62:         return ""

64: def get_session_id():
65:     try:
66:         import ctypes
67:         sid = ctypes.c_ulong()
68:         if ctypes.windll.kernel32.ProcessIdToSessionId(ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetCurrentProcessId(), ctypes.byref(sid)):
69:             return sid.value
70:     except:
71:         pass
72:     return 1

74: def get_desktop_name():
75:     try:
76:         import ctypes
77:         h_desk = ctypes.windll.user32.GetThreadDesktop(ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetCurrentThreadId())
78:         name = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
79:         size = ctypes.c_ulong(256)
80:         if ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(h_desk, 2, name, size, None):
81:             return name.value.lower()
82:     except:
83:         pass
84:     return "default"

86: def is_secure_desktop():
87:     """Kiểm tra xem thread hiện tại có đang ở Secure Desktop (UAC/Winlogon) không.
88:     Trả về True nếu đang ở Secure Desktop và KHÔNG thể chụp màn hình bình thường."""
89:     try:
90:         # Mở Input Desktop với quyền đọc tối thiểu (DESKTOP_READOBJECTS = 0x0001)
91:         hdesk = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, 0x0001)
92:         if not hdesk:
93:             # Không mở được Input Desktop → đang bị Secure Desktop lock
94:             return True
95:         name = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
96:         ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(hdesk, 2, name, ctypes.sizeof(name), None)
97:         ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(hdesk)
98:         desktop_name = name.value.lower()
99:         # "default" là desktop bình thường; bất kỳ tên nào khác (vd: "winlogon", "secure") là Secure Desktop
100:         return desktop_name not in ("default", "")
101:     except:
102:         return False
103: 
104: def check_desktop_change():
105:     """
106:     Checks the status of active input desktop relative to the current thread.
107:     Returns (needs_switch, is_blocked)
108:     - needs_switch: True if the active input desktop is different from the current thread desktop,
109:                     and we CAN access/switch to it.
110:     - is_blocked: True if the active input desktop cannot be accessed (e.g. secure desktop for normal user).
111:     """
112:     try:
113:         import ctypes
114:         # Try multiple access levels to open the Input Desktop.
115:         # SYSTEM processes (headless agent) can access Secure Desktop with higher rights.
116:         # On Windows 11, when an elevated (Administrator) window has focus, OpenInputDesktop fails
117:         # with Access Denied (5) if requesting high access rights (GENERIC_ALL/GENERIC_READ/READOBJECTS).
118:         # We include DESKTOP_ENUMERATE (0x0040) and zero-mask (0) which succeed to let us read the desktop name.
119:         h_input = None
120:         for access_mask in [0x02000000, 0x80000000, 0x0001, 0x0040, 0]:
121:             h_input = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, access_mask)
122:             if h_input:
123:                 break
124:         
125:         if not h_input:
126:             # Truly blocked (e.g. secure desktop is active and we have no rights to open it even with 0 access)
127:             return False, True
128:             
129:         # Get active input desktop name
130:         name_input = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
131:         ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(h_input, 2, name_input, ctypes.sizeof(name_input), None)
132:         ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_input)
133:         input_name = name_input.value.lower()
134:         
135:         # 2. Get current thread desktop name
136:         thread_name = get_desktop_name()
137:             
138:         if input_name != thread_name:
139:             try:
140:                 # We try to open the target desktop with required access to switch to it
141:                 h_target = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenDesktopW(input_name, 0, False, 0x02000000)
142:                 if h_target:
143:                     ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_target)
144:                     return True, False
145:             except:
146:                 pass
147:             return False, True
148:             
149:         return False, False
150:     except Exception as e:
151:         print(f"[DesktopCheck] Error: {e}")
152:         return False, False

154: def is_machine_domain_joined():
155:     debug_messages = []
156:     
157:     # Check if Windows Server first (Server editions always require Ctrl+Alt+Del by default)
158:     try:
159:         import ctypes
160:         is_server_metric = ctypes.windll.user32.GetSystemMetrics(89)
161:         debug_messages.append(f"GetSystemMetrics(89)={is_server_metric}")
162:         if is_server_metric != 0:
163:             return True, f"Windows Server detection (SystemMetrics 89): {is_server_metric}"
164:     except Exception as e:
165:         debug_messages.append(f"GetSystemMetrics(89) check failed: {e}")
166: 
167:     try:
168:         import winreg
169:         with winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion") as key:
170:             install_type, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "InstallationType")
171:             debug_messages.append(f"OS InstallationType={install_type}")
172:             if install_type and "server" in install_type.lower():
173:                 return True, f"Windows Server detection (registry): {install_type}"
174:     except Exception as e:
175:         debug_messages.append(f"Server check (registry) failed: {e}")
176: 
177:     try:
178:         import platform
179:         win_ver = platform.win32_ver()
180:         release_ver = platform.release()
181:         debug_messages.append(f"platform.win32_ver={win_ver}, platform.release={release_ver}")
182:         if "server" in win_ver[1].lower() or "server" in release_ver.lower():
183:             return True, f"Windows Server detection (platform): win_ver={win_ver}, release={release_ver}"
184:     except Exception as e:
185:         debug_messages.append(f"Server check (platform) failed: {e}")
186:         
187:     # Method 1: Pure ctypes NetGetJoinInformation (Official Windows API, no dependencies)
188:     try:
189:         import ctypes
190:         netapi32 = ctypes.windll.netapi32
191:         name_ptr = ctypes.c_wchar_p()
192:         join_status = ctypes.c_int()
193:         res = netapi32.NetGetJoinInformation(None, ctypes.byref(name_ptr), ctypes.byref(join_status))
194:         if res == 0:
195:             name = name_ptr.value
196:             status = join_status.value
197:             netapi32.NetApiBufferFree(name_ptr)
198:             debug_messages.append(f"ctypes NetGetJoinInformation: name={name}, status={status}")
199:             if status == 3: # NetSetupDomainName = 3
200:                 return True, f"ctypes NetGetJoinInformation: {name}"
201:         else:
202:             debug_messages.append(f"ctypes NetGetJoinInformation failed: error_code={res}")
203:     except Exception as e:
204:         debug_messages.append(f"ctypes NetGetJoinInformation exception: {e}")
205: 
206:     # Method 2: Pure ctypes GetComputerNameExW (DnsDomain = 2)
207:     try:
208:         import ctypes
209:         size = ctypes.c_ulong(1024)
210:         buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(1024)
211:         if ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetComputerNameExW(2, buf, ctypes.byref(size)):
212:             dns_domain = buf.value.strip()
213:             debug_messages.append(f"ctypes GetComputerNameExW: domain={dns_domain}")
214:             if dns_domain and len(dns_domain) > 0:
215:                 return True, f"ctypes GetComputerNameExW: {dns_domain}"
216:         else:
217:             debug_messages.append("ctypes GetComputerNameExW failed")
218:     except Exception as e:
219:         debug_messages.append(f"ctypes GetComputerNameExW exception: {e}")
220: 
221:     # Method 3: win32net
222:     try:
223:         import win32net
224:         name, join_status = win32net.NetGetJoinInformation()
225:         debug_messages.append(f"win32net name={name}, status={join_status}")
226:         if join_status == 3: # NetSetupDomainName = 3
227:             return True, f"win32net: {name}"
228:     except Exception as e:
229:         debug_messages.append(f"win32net failed: {e}")
230: 
231:     # Method 4: Check registry for Tcpip Parameters Domain
232:     try:
233:         import winreg
234:         with winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"System\CurrentControlSet\Services\Tcpip\Parameters") as key:
235:             domain, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "Domain")
236:             debug_messages.append(f"Tcpip Domain reg={domain}")
237:             if domain and len(domain.strip()) > 0:
238:                 return True, f"registry (Tcpip): {domain}"
239:     except Exception as e:
240:         debug_messages.append(f"Tcpip Domain reg failed: {e}")
241: 
242:     # Method 5: Check environment variables
243:     if "USERDNSDOMAIN" in os.environ:
244:         debug_messages.append(f"USERDNSDOMAIN env={os.environ['USERDNSDOMAIN']}")
245:         return True, f"env (USERDNSDOMAIN): {os.environ['USERDNSDOMAIN']}"
246:     else:
247:         debug_messages.append("USERDNSDOMAIN env not found")
248:         
249:     return False, f"Not domain joined. Debug details: {'; '.join(debug_messages)}"

251: def host_type_password(password):
252:     import time
253:     import ctypes
254:     
255:     # 1. Type password characters via Unicode SendInput
256:     for char in password:
257:         inp_down = INPUT()
258:         inp_down.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD
259:         inp_down.union.ki.wVk = 0
260:         inp_down.union.ki.wScan = ord(char)
261:         inp_down.union.ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_UNICODE
262:         inp_down.union.ki.time = 0
263:         inp_down.union.ki.dwExtraInfo = None
264:         
265:         inp_up = INPUT()
266:         inp_up.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD
267:         inp_up.union.ki.wVk = 0
268:         inp_up.union.ki.wScan = ord(char)
269:         inp_up.union.ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_UNICODE | KEYEVENTF_KEYUP
270:         inp_up.union.ki.time = 0
271:         inp_up.union.ki.dwExtraInfo = None
272:         
273:         ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp_down), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
274:         time.sleep(0.01)
275:         ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp_up), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
276:         time.sleep(0.01)
277:         
278:     # 2. Press Enter to submit (VK_RETURN = 0x0D)
279:     time.sleep(0.1)
280:     inp_enter_down = INPUT()
281:     inp_enter_down.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD
282:     inp_enter_down.union.ki.wVk = 0x0D
283:     inp_enter_down.union.ki.wScan = 0
284:     inp_enter_down.union.ki.dwFlags = 0
285:     inp_enter_down.union.ki.time = 0
286:     inp_enter_down.union.ki.dwExtraInfo = None
287:     
288:     inp_enter_up = INPUT()
289:     inp_enter_up.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD
290:     inp_enter_up.union.ki.wVk = 0x0D
291:     inp_enter_up.union.ki.wScan = 0
292:     inp_enter_up.union.ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_KEYUP
293:     inp_enter_up.union.ki.time = 0
294:     inp_enter_up.union.ki.dwExtraInfo = None
295:     
296:     ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp_enter_down), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
297:     time.sleep(0.01)
298:     ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp_enter_up), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))

300: def set_windows_graphics_effects(enabled=True):
301:     if sys.platform != "win32":
302:         return
303:     try:
304:         import ctypes
305:         # Font Smoothing (ClearType)
306:         ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(0x004B, 1 if enabled else 0, None, 3)
307:         
308:         # Drag Full Windows
309:         ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(0x0025, 1 if enabled else 0, None, 3)
310:         
311:         # Menu Animation
312:         ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(0x1003, 1 if enabled else 0, None, 3)
313:         
314:         # UI Effects
315:         ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(0x103E, 1 if enabled else 0, None, 3)
316:         
317:         # Window Animations (iMinAnimate)
318:         class ANIMATIONINFO(ctypes.Structure):
319:             _fields_ = [("cbSize", ctypes.c_uint), ("iMinAnimate", ctypes.c_int)]
320:         info = ANIMATIONINFO()
321:         info.cbSize = ctypes.sizeof(ANIMATIONINFO)
322:         info.iMinAnimate = 1 if enabled else 0
323:         ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(0x0049, info.cbSize, ctypes.byref(info), 3)
324:         
325:         print(f"[Host] Set Windows graphics effects to: {enabled}")
326:     except Exception as e:
327:         print(f"[Host] Error setting Windows graphics effects: {e}")

333: class HostMixin:
334:     def ensure_input_thread_desktop(self, force=False):
335:         if getattr(self, 'is_headless', False) is False and sys.platform != "win32":
336:             return
337:             
338:         now = time.time()
339:         last_check = getattr(self, '_last_input_desktop_check', 0)
340:         if not force and (now - last_check < 0.2):
341:             return
342:         self._last_input_desktop_check = now
343:         
344:         try:
345:             h_input = None
346:             # Try specific desktop rights (0x01FF) first, as it is more likely to succeed for SYSTEM than GENERIC_ALL
347:             for access_mask in [0x01FF, 0x02000000, 0x80000000, 0x0001, 0]:
348:                 try:
349:                     h_input = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, access_mask)
350:                     if h_input:
351:                         break
352:                 except:
353:                     pass
354:                     
355:             if h_input:
356:                 name_input = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
357:                 ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(h_input, 2, name_input, ctypes.sizeof(name_input), None)
358:                 
359:                 h_thread = ctypes.windll.user32.GetThreadDesktop(ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetCurrentThreadId())
360:                 name_thread = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
361:                 ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(h_thread, 2, name_thread, ctypes.sizeof(name_thread), None)
362:                 
363:                 if name_input.value.lower() != name_thread.value.lower():
364:                     print(f"[Host Input] Desktop changed from {name_thread.value} to {name_input.value}. Switching input thread...")
365:                     result = ctypes.windll.user32.SetThreadDesktop(h_input)
366:                     if not result:
367:                         print(f"[Host Input] SetThreadDesktop failed. Error code: {ctypes.get_last_error()}")
368:                 ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_input)
369:         except Exception as e:
370:             print(f"[Host Input] Error in ensure_input_thread_desktop: {e}")
371: 
372:     def start_host_server(self):
373:         global BOUND_PORT
374:         bound = False
375:         for port in PORTS_TO_TRY:
376:             # Check if port is already in use by another instance
377:             test_sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
378:             test_sock.settimeout(0.5)
379:             in_use = (test_sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) == 0)
380:             test_sock.close()
381:             if in_use:
382:                 print(f"[Host] Port {port} is already in use. Skipping.")
383:                 continue
384: 
385:             try:
386:                 # Try IPv6 Dual-Stack first (binds to both IPv6 and IPv4)
387:                 try:
388:                     if hasattr(socket, 'AF_INET6'):
389:                         self.server_socket = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET6, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
390:                         self.server_socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
391:                         if hasattr(socket, 'IPPROTO_IPV6') and hasattr(socket, 'IPV6_V6ONLY'):
392:                             try: self.server_socket.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IPV6, socket.IPV6_V6ONLY, 0)
393:                             except: pass
394:                         self.server_socket.bind(("", port))
395:                     else:
396:                         raise Exception("No IPv6")
397:                 except Exception:
398:                     self.server_socket = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
399:                     self.server_socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
400:                     self.server_socket.bind(('0.0.0.0', port))
401:                     
402:                 BOUND_PORT = port
403:                 import core.config; core.config.BOUND_PORT = port
404:                 import core.network_manager; core.network_manager.BOUND_PORT = port
405:                 self.server_socket.listen(5)
406:                 print(f"[Host] TCP server successfully listening on port {BOUND_PORT} (Dual-Stack)...")
407:                 bound = True
408:                 break
409:             except Exception as e:
410:                 print(f"[Host] Failed to bind to port {port}: {e}")
411:                 continue
412:                 
413:         if not bound:
414:             self.after(0, lambda: self.show_custom_error(_("Lỗi hệ thống"), _("Không thể chạy server! Các cổng mạng đều bị chiếm dụng hoặc bị chặn bởi Tường lửa.\nVui lòng kiểm tra lại cấu hình mạng hoặc tắt bớt ứng dụng chiếm cổng.")))
415:             self.update_status(_("Lỗi khởi động Server"))
416:             return
417:             
418:         # Spawn the socket accept loop in a separate thread
419:         threading.Thread(target=self.host_accept_loop, daemon=True).start()
420: 
421:     def host_accept_loop(self):
422:         while self.running_server:
423:             try:
424:                 conn, addr = self.server_socket.accept()
425:                 print(f"[Host] Connection attempt from {addr[0]}:{addr[1]}")
426:                 threading.Thread(target=self.handle_host_handshake, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
427:             except Exception as e:
428:                 if not self.running_server:
429:                     break
430:                 print(f"[Host] Warning: accept() failed with error: {e}")
431:                 time.sleep(0.1)
432:                 continue
433:                 
434:     def show_host_connection_border(self):
435:         try:
436:             self.hide_host_connection_border()
437:             
438:             import tkinter as tk
439:             self.host_border_wins = []
440:             
441:             w = self.winfo_screenwidth()
442:             h = self.winfo_screenheight()
443:             self._last_border_w = w
444:             self._last_border_h = h
445:             
446:             thickness = 5
447:             color = "#FF69B4"
448:             
449:             rects = [
450:                 (0, 0, w, thickness),           # top
451:                 (0, h - thickness, w, thickness), # bottom
452:                 (0, 0, thickness, h),           # left
453:                 (w - thickness, 0, thickness, h)  # right
454:             ]
455:             
456:             for x, y, rw, rh in rects:
457:                 win = tk.Toplevel(self)
458:                 win.overrideredirect(True)
459:                 win.attributes("-topmost", True)
460:                 win.attributes("-alpha", 0.8)
461:                 win.configure(bg=color)
462:                 win.geometry(f"{rw}x{rh}+{x}+{y}")
463:                 win.update_idletasks()
464:                 try:
465:                     import ctypes
466:                     hwnd = ctypes.windll.user32.GetParent(win.winfo_id())
467:                     if not hwnd:
468:                         hwnd = win.winfo_id()
469:                     style = ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowLongW(hwnd, -20)
470:                     ctypes.windll.user32.SetWindowLongW(hwnd, -20, style | 0x00000020)
471:                 except:
472:                     pass
473:                 self.host_border_wins.append(win)
474:                 
475:             if not getattr(self, '_tracking_border_res', False):
476:                 self._tracking_border_res = True
477:                 self._check_border_resolution()
478:                 
479:         except Exception as e:
480:             print(f"[Host] Lỗi tạo viền hồng kết nối: {e}")
481: 
482:     def _check_border_resolution(self):

147:                     ctypes.byref(si),
148:                     ctypes.byref(pi)
149:                 )
150:                 
151:                 if not res:
152:                     log(f"CreateProcessWithTokenW cũng thất bại với lỗi: {ctypes.get_last_error()}")
153:                     raise e
154:                 
155:                 ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(pi.hProcess)
156:                 ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(pi.hThread)
157:                 return pi.dwProcessId
158:             except Exception as fb_err:
159:                 log(f"Lỗi khi chạy fallback: {fb_err}")
160:                 raise e
161:         else:
162:             raise e
163: 
164: 
165: def configure_uac_registry():
166:     """
167:     Configure registry to disable UAC secure desktop switching (PromptOnSecureDesktop = 0).
168:     This ensures that on virtual machines (or when GPU display drivers are limited),
169:     UAC prompt windows are displayed on the default user desktop where they can be captured
170:     and controlled without session freeze or connection loss.
171:     """
172:     try:
173:         import winreg
174:         path = r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\System"
175:         try:
176:             key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, path, 0, winreg.KEY_ALL_ACCESS)
177:         except WindowsError:
178:             key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, path, 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
179:         
180:         winreg.SetValueEx(key, "PromptOnSecureDesktop", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 1)
181:         winreg.SetValueEx(key, "SoftwareSASGeneration", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 3)
182:         winreg.CloseKey(key)
183:         log("Đã cấu hình thành công Registry (PromptOnSecureDesktop=1, SoftwareSASGeneration=3).")
184:     except Exception as e:
185:         log(f"Thất bại khi cấu hình Registry: {e}")
186: 
187: def get_active_session_id():
188:     try:
189:         sessions = win32ts.WTSEnumerateSessions(win32ts.WTS_CURRENT_SERVER_HANDLE, 1, 0)
190:         for s in sessions:
191:             if s['State'] == 0:  # WTSActive
192:                 return s['SessionId']
193:     except Exception as e:
194:         log(f"Lỗi liệt kê các session WTS: {e}")
195:     # Fallback
196:     return win32ts.WTSGetActiveConsoleSessionId()
197: 
198: def is_logon_ui_running(session_id):
199:     try:
200:         procs = win32ts.WTSEnumerateProcesses(win32ts.WTS_CURRENT_SERVER_HANDLE)
201:         for p in procs:
202:             if p[0] == session_id and p[2].lower() == "logonui.exe":
203:                 return True
204:     except Exception as e:
205:         log(f"Lỗi kiểm tra LogonUI: {e}")
206:     return False
207: 
208: def find_winlogon_pid(session_id):
209:     try:
210:         procs = win32ts.WTSEnumerateProcesses(win32ts.WTS_CURRENT_SERVER_HANDLE)
211:         for p in procs:
212:             if p[0] == session_id and p[2].lower() == "winlogon.exe":
213:                 return p[1]
214:     except Exception as e:
215:         log(f"Lỗi liệt kê các tiến trình: {e}")
216:     return None
217: 
218: def get_executable_to_run():
219:     # Prefer compiled standalone Nuitka binary
220:     candidates = [
221:         os.path.join(app_dir, "dist_nuitka", "app.dist", "RemoteDesktopP2P.exe"),
222:         os.path.join(app_dir, "dist", "RemoteDesktopP2P.exe"),
223:         os.path.join(app_dir, "dist_standalone", "app.dist", "RemoteDesktopP2P.exe"),
224:         os.path.join(app_dir, "dist", "RemoteDesktopP2P", "RemoteDesktopP2P.exe"),
225:         os.path.join(app_dir, "dist", "app.exe"),
226:         os.path.join(app_dir, "RemoteDesktopP2P.exe"),
227:         os.path.join(app_dir, "app.exe"),
228:     ]
229:     for c in candidates:
230:         if os.path.exists(c):
231:             log(f"Đã tìm thấy file thực thi biên dịch của Agent: {c}")
232:             return c, f'"{c}" --headless'
233: 
234:     # Fallback to source
235:     python_exe = os.path.join(app_dir, ".venv", "Scripts", "python.exe")
236:     app_py = os.path.join(app_dir, "app.py")
237:     if os.path.exists(python_exe) and os.path.exists(app_py):
238:         log(f"Sử dụng nguồn Python dự phòng để chạy Agent bằng: {python_exe}")
239:         return python_exe, f'"{python_exe}" "{app_py}" --headless'
240: 
241:     log("Lỗi: Không tìm thấy file thực thi hoặc file nguồn app.py!")
242:     return None, None
243: 
244: def spawn_agent(session_id, is_logged_in, is_screen_locked):
245:     exe_path, cmd_line = get_executable_to_run()
246:     if not exe_path:
247:         return None
248: 
249:     h_token = None
250:     desktop = "winsta0\\default"
251: 
252:     # We ALWAYS use the winlogon token to run the agent as SYSTEM in the user session.
253:     # This allows the agent to dynamically switch between desktops (winsta0\default and winsta0\winlogon)
254:     # and control administrative apps / UAC prompts without permission blocks.
255:     winlogon_pid = find_winlogon_pid(session_id)
256:     if not winlogon_pid:
257:         log(f"Không tìm thấy winlogon.exe trong session {session_id}. Không thể chạy Agent.")
258:         return None
259: 
260:     try:
261:         # Open winlogon and its token
262:         h_winlogon = win32api.OpenProcess(
263:             win32con.PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, False, winlogon_pid
264:         )
265:         h_token = win32security.OpenProcessToken(
266:             h_winlogon, win32con.TOKEN_DUPLICATE | win32con.TOKEN_QUERY | win32con.TOKEN_ASSIGN_PRIMARY
267:         )
268:         win32api.CloseHandle(h_winlogon)
269:         
270:         # Target appropriate initial desktop based on active state
271:         if not is_logged_in:
272:             desktop = "winsta0\\winlogon"
273:             log(f"Không có người dùng đăng nhập. Nhắm tới desktop Winlogon cho session {session_id} bằng token SYSTEM")
274:         elif is_screen_locked:
275:             desktop = "winsta0\\winlogon"
276:             log(f"Đang nhắm tới desktop màn hình khóa (Winlogon) cho session {session_id} bằng token SYSTEM")
277:         else:
278:             desktop = "winsta0\\default"
279:             log(f"Đang nhắm tới desktop người dùng mặc định cho session {session_id} bằng token SYSTEM")
280:     except Exception as e:
281:         log(f"Thất bại khi lấy token Winlogon: {e}")
282:         return None
283: 
284:     if h_token:
285:         try:
286:             h_token_dup = win32security.DuplicateTokenEx(
287:                 h_token,
288:                 win32security.SecurityImpersonation,
289:                 win32con.TOKEN_ALL_ACCESS,
290:                 win32security.TokenPrimary
291:             )
292:             win32api.CloseHandle(h_token)
293: 
294:             # Run process in active user session context with robust fallback
295:             dwProcessId = create_process_robust(
296:                 h_token_dup,
297:                 exe_path,
298:                 cmd_line,
299:                 desktop,
300:                 win32con.NORMAL_PRIORITY_CLASS | win32process.CREATE_NO_WINDOW,
301:                 os.path.dirname(exe_path)
302:             )
303:             win32api.CloseHandle(h_token_dup)
304: 
305:             log(f"Đã khởi chạy Agent thành công với PID {dwProcessId} trên desktop {desktop}")
306:             return dwProcessId
307:         except Exception as e:
308:             log(f"Khởi chạy Agent thất bại toàn diện: {e}")
309:             try: win32api.CloseHandle(h_token_dup)
310:             except: pass
311:     return None
312: 
313: def spawn_clipboard_agent(session_id):
314:     """
315:     Spawn Clipboard Agent ở quyền User thường.
316:     """
317:     exe_path, cmd_line = get_executable_to_run()
318:     if not exe_path:
319:         return None
320:     
321:     cmd_line = cmd_line.replace("--headless", "--clipboard-agent")
322: 
323:     h_user_token = None
324:     try:
325:         h_user_token = win32ts.WTSQueryUserToken(session_id)
326:     except Exception as e:
327:         log(f"Thất bại khi truy vấn token người dùng cho Clipboard Agent (session {session_id}): {e}")
328:         return None
329: 
330:     if h_user_token:
331:         try:
332:             h_token_dup = win32security.DuplicateTokenEx(
333:                 h_user_token,
334:                 win32security.SecurityImpersonation,
335:                 win32con.TOKEN_ALL_ACCESS,
336:                 win32security.TokenPrimary
337:             )
338:             
339:             environment = win32profile.CreateEnvironmentBlock(h_token_dup, False)
340:             
341:             creation_flags = win32process.CREATE_UNICODE_ENVIRONMENT | win32process.CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE
342: 
343:             dwProcessId = create_process_robust(
344:                 h_token_dup,
345:                 exe_path,
346:                 cmd_line,
347:                 "winsta0\\default",
348:                 creation_flags,
349:                 os.path.dirname(exe_path),
350:                 environment
351:             )
352:             win32api.CloseHandle(h_token_dup)
353: 
354:             log(f"Đã khởi chạy Clipboard Agent với PID {dwProcessId} trên winsta0\\default (quyền User thường)")
355:             return dwProcessId
356:         except Exception as e:
357:             log(f"Khởi chạy Clipboard Agent thất bại toàn diện: {e}")
358:             try: win32api.CloseHandle(h_token_dup)
359:             except: pass
360:     return None
361: 
362: 
363: def trigger_sas_system():
364:     try:
365:         import winreg
366:         import ctypes
367:         
368:         try:
369:             key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\System", 0, winreg.KEY_ALL_ACCESS)
370:         except WindowsError:
371:             key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\System", 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
372:         winreg.SetValueEx(key, "SoftwareSASGeneration", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 3)
373:         winreg.CloseKey(key)
374:         log("Đã cấu hình SoftwareSASGeneration = 3 trong Registry.")
375:     except Exception as e:
376:         log(f"Lỗi khi cấu hình SoftwareSASGeneration trong service: {e}")
377: 
378:     try:
379:         sas_dll = ctypes.windll.LoadLibrary("sas.dll")
380:         sas_dll.SendSAS.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int]
381:         sas_dll.SendSAS.restype = None
382:         sas_dll.SendSAS(0)
383:         log("SendSAS(0) thực thi thành công từ Service Session 0.")
384:     except Exception as e:
385:         log(f"Lỗi khi gọi SendSAS trong service: {e}")
386: 
387: def spawn_taskmgr_system():
388:     try:
389:         active_session_id = get_active_session_id()
390:         if active_session_id == 0xFFFFFFFF or active_session_id == -1:
391:             return
392: 
393:         is_screen_locked = is_logon_ui_running(active_session_id)
394:         if is_screen_locked:
395:             log("Màn hình đang khóa, bỏ qua việc chạy Task Manager.")
396:             return
397: 
398:         try:
399:             h_user_token = win32ts.WTSQueryUserToken(active_session_id)
400:         except Exception as e:
401:             log(f"Thất bại khi truy vấn token người dùng cho Task Manager (session {active_session_id}): {e}")
402:             return
403: 
404:         try:
405:             elevation_type = win32security.GetTokenInformation(h_user_token, win32security.TokenElevationType)
406:             if elevation_type == 3: # TokenElevationTypeLimited
407:                 linked_token = win32security.GetTokenInformation(h_user_token, win32security.TokenLinkedToken)
408:                 if linked_token:
409:                     win32api.CloseHandle(h_user_token)
410:                     h_user_token = linked_token
411:         except Exception as e:
412:             log(f"Thất bại khi lấy linked token: {e}")
413: 
414:         if h_user_token:
415:             h_token_dup = win32security.DuplicateTokenEx(
416:                 h_user_token,
417:                 win32security.SecurityImpersonation,
418:                 win32con.TOKEN_ALL_ACCESS,
419:                 win32security.TokenPrimary
420:             )
421:             win32api.CloseHandle(h_user_token)
422: 
423:             dwProcessId = create_process_robust(
424:                 h_token_dup,
425:                 taskmgr_exe,
426:                 None,
427:                 desktop,
428:                 win32con.NORMAL_PRIORITY_CLASS,
429:                 sys32
430:             )
431:             win32api.CloseHandle(h_token_dup)
432:             log(f"Đã khởi chạy thành công Task Manager với PID {dwProcessId} trên desktop {desktop} dưới quyền SYSTEM")
433:     except Exception as e:
434:         log(f"Thất bại khi chạy Task Manager: {e}")
435: 
436: def service_events_listener_thread():
437:     # Setup security attributes with NULL DACL to allow user processes to trigger
438:     sa = win32security.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES()
439:     sa.bInheritHandle = 1
440:     sd = win32security.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR()
441:     sd.Initialize()
442:     sd.SetSecurityDescriptorDacl(True, None, False)
443:     sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
444: 
445:     try:
446:         h_sas_event = win32event.CreateEvent(sa, False, False, "Global\\AntigravityP2P_SAS_Event")
447:         h_taskmgr_event = win32event.CreateEvent(sa, False, False, "Global\\AntigravityP2P_TaskMgr_Event")
448:     except Exception as e:
449:         log(f"Thất bại khi tạo event: {e}")
450:         return
451: 
452:     log("Luồng lắng nghe sự kiện của Service đã khởi động và đang chờ...")
453:     handles = [h_sas_event, h_taskmgr_event]
454:     while True:
455:         rc = win32event.WaitForMultipleObjects(handles, False, win32event.INFINITE)
456:         if rc == win32event.WAIT_OBJECT_0:
457:             log("Nhận được tín hiệu sự kiện SAS từ Agent. Đang kích hoạt SendSAS.")
458:             trigger_sas_system()
459:         elif rc == win32event.WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1:
460:             log("Nhận được tín hiệu sự kiện TaskMgr từ Agent. Đang kích hoạt Task Manager.")
461:             spawn_taskmgr_system()
462: 
463: def terminate_process_with_pid(pid):
464:     if not pid:
465:         return
466:     log(f"Đang cố gắng dừng tiến trình {pid}")
467:     success = False
468:     try:
469:         h_proc = win32api.OpenProcess(win32con.PROCESS_TERMINATE, False, pid)
470:         win32api.TerminateProcess(h_proc, 0)
471:         win32api.CloseHandle(h_proc)
472:         log(f"Đã dừng tiến trình {pid} qua Windows API.")
473:         success = True
474:     except Exception as e:
475:         log(f"Không thể dừng Agent {pid} qua Windows API: {e}")
476: 
477:     if not success:
478:         try:
479:             import subprocess
480:             subprocess.run(f"taskkill /F /PID {pid}", shell=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
481:             log(f"Đã dừng tiến trình {pid} qua lệnh taskkill.")
482:             success = True
483:         except Exception as e:
484:             log(f"Không thể dừng Agent {pid} qua lệnh taskkill: {e}")
485: 
486: def is_process_alive(pid):
487:     """Check if a process with the given PID is still running."""
488:     if not pid:
489:         return False
490:     try:
491:         h_proc = win32api.OpenProcess(win32con.PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, False, pid)
492:         if h_proc:
493:             exit_code = win32process.GetExitCodeProcess(h_proc)
494:             win32api.CloseHandle(h_proc)
495:             # STILL_ACTIVE = 259
496:             if exit_code != 259:
497:                 log(f"[DEBUG] Tiến trình {pid} đã thoát với mã: {exit_code}")
498:             return exit_code == 259
499:     except Exception:
500:         pass
501:     return False
502: 
503: def main():
504:     enable_all_privileges()
505:     log("Vòng lặp service Easy Remote Desktop Agent bắt đầu chạy.")
506:     configure_uac_registry()
507:     
508:     # Clean up any lingering agent processes
509:     try:
510:         import subprocess
511:         subprocess.run("taskkill /F /IM RemoteDesktopP2P.exe", shell=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
512:         log("Đã dọn dẹp các tiến trình RemoteDesktopP2P.exe còn sót lại.")
513:     except Exception as e:
514:         log(f"Lỗi khi dọn dẹp tiến trình lúc khởi động: {e}")
515: 
516:     # Start Service Events Listener thread
517:     import threading
518:     t = threading.Thread(target=service_events_listener_thread, daemon=True)
519:     t.start()
520: 
521:     current_agent_pid = None
522:     gui_agent_pid = None
523:     clipboard_agent_pid = None
524:     last_session_id = None
525: 
526:     while True:
527:         try:
528:             active_session_id = get_active_session_id()
529:             if active_session_id == 0xFFFFFFFF or active_session_id == -1:
530:                 time.sleep(5)
531:                 continue
532: 
533:             # Check if user is logged in
534:             is_logged_in = False
535:             try:
536:                 h_token = win32ts.WTSQueryUserToken(active_session_id)
537:                 is_logged_in = True
538:                 win32api.CloseHandle(h_token)
539:             except Exception:
540:                 pass
541: 
542:             # Check if screen is locked (LogonUI is running)
543:             is_screen_locked = is_logon_ui_running(active_session_id)
544: 
545:             session_changed = (last_session_id is not None and last_session_id != active_session_id)
546: 
547:             if session_changed:
548:                 log(f"Session ID đã thay đổi từ {last_session_id} sang {active_session_id}. Đang tắt Agent để chuyển session.")
549:                 if current_agent_pid:
550:                     terminate_process_with_pid(current_agent_pid)
551:                     current_agent_pid = None
552:                 if gui_agent_pid:
553:                     terminate_process_with_pid(gui_agent_pid)
554:                     gui_agent_pid = None
555:                 if clipboard_agent_pid:
556:                     terminate_process_with_pid(clipboard_agent_pid)
557:                     clipboard_agent_pid = None
558: 
559:             last_session_id = active_session_id
560: 
561:             if current_agent_pid and not is_process_alive(current_agent_pid):
562:                 log(f"Agent với PID {current_agent_pid} đã dừng hoạt động. Sẽ khởi chạy lại.")
563:                 current_agent_pid = None
564: 
565:             agent_running = False
566:             for desktop_name in ['default', 'winlogon']:
567:                 mutex_name = f"Global\\AntigravityP2PRemoteDesktopAppMutex_1_{active_session_id}_{desktop_name}"
568:                 try:
569:                     h_mutex = win32event.OpenMutex(win32con.SYNCHRONIZE, False, mutex_name)
570:                     win32api.CloseHandle(h_mutex)
571:                     agent_running = True
572:                     break
573:                 except Exception as e:
574:                     err_code = 0
575:                     if hasattr(e, 'winerror'):
576:                         err_code = e.winerror
577:                     elif hasattr(e, 'args') and len(e.args) > 0:
578:                         err_code = e.args[0]
579:                     
580:                     if err_code != 2:
581:                         agent_running = True
582:                         break
583: 
584:             if not agent_running:
585:                 if current_agent_pid and is_process_alive(current_agent_pid):
586:                     log(f"Mutex của Agent chưa sẵn sàng nhưng tiến trình {current_agent_pid} vẫn đang khởi động. Chờ đợi...")
587:                 else:
588:                     log(f"Không tìm thấy Mutex của Agent cho session {active_session_id}. Đang khởi chạy Agent mới (LoggedIn={is_logged_in}, Locked={is_screen_locked})")
589:                     pid = spawn_agent(active_session_id, is_logged_in, is_screen_locked)
590:                     if pid:
591:                         current_agent_pid = pid
592: 
593:             # Spawn or check Clipboard Agent (only when user is logged in and not locked)
594:             if is_logged_in and not is_screen_locked:
595:                 if clipboard_agent_pid and not is_process_alive(clipboard_agent_pid):
596:                     log(f"Clipboard Agent với PID {clipboard_agent_pid} đã dừng hoạt động. Sẽ khởi chạy lại.")
597:                     clipboard_agent_pid = None
598: 
599:                 clipboard_agent_running = False
600:                 mutex_name_global_cb = f"Global\\AntigravityP2PClipboardAgentMutex_{active_session_id}"
601:                 mutex_name_session_cb = f"Session\\{active_session_id}\\AntigravityP2PClipboardAgentMutex_{active_session_id}"
602:                 
603:                 try:
604:                     h_mutex = win32event.OpenMutex(win32con.SYNCHRONIZE, False, mutex_name_global_cb)
605:                     win32api.CloseHandle(h_mutex)
606:                     clipboard_agent_running = True
607:                 except Exception as e:
608:                     if getattr(e, 'winerror', 0) != 2:
609:                         clipboard_agent_running = True
610:                         
611:                 if not clipboard_agent_running:
612:                     try:
613:                         h_mutex = win32event.OpenMutex(win32con.SYNCHRONIZE, False, mutex_name_session_cb)
614:                         win32api.CloseHandle(h_mutex)
615:                         clipboard_agent_running = True
616:                     except Exception as e:
617:                         if getattr(e, 'winerror', 0) != 2:
618:                             clipboard_agent_running = True
619:

167:     Configure registry to disable UAC secure desktop switching (PromptOnSecureDesktop = 0).
168:     This ensures that on virtual machines (or when GPU display drivers are limited),
169:     UAC prompt windows are displayed on the default user desktop where they can be captured

========== MODEL ==========
Tôi đã tìm ra **nguyên nhân gốc** của vấn đề. Đây là phân tích:

---

## Nguyên nhân: `PromptOnSecureDesktop` đang bị đặt = **1** thay vì **0**

Trong file [windows_service_loop.py](file:///d:/Data/AG/remote_desktop/windows_service_loop.py#L180), dòng 180:

```python
winreg.SetValueEx(key, "PromptOnSecureDesktop", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 1)  # ← BUG: value = 1
```

**Docstring viết rõ mục đích là "disable UAC secure desktop" (tắt Secure Desktop)**, nhưng code lại đặt giá trị = `1`, tức là **BẬT Secure Desktop**. Đúng ra phải đặt = `0`.

### Khi `PromptOnSecureDesktop = 1` (mặc định Windows 11):
1. UAC prompt hiển thị trên **Secure Desktop** (desktop riêng biệt tên `"winlogon"`)
2. Desktop thường (`"default"`) bị **dim/freeze** hoàn toàn
3. **`mss.grab()`** không thể chụp được Secure Desktop → agent gửi tín hiệu `switching_desktop` cho client
4. **`SendInput()`** không thể gửi input tới Secure Desktop từ thread đang ở desktop `"default"`
5. Agent chạy dưới token SYSTEM **có thể** `OpenInputDesktop` và `SetThreadDesktop` sang Secure Desktop, nhưng code có race condition và throttle 12 giây, gây treo

### Khi `PromptOnSecureDesktop = 0`:
- UAC prompt hiện trên **desktop `"default"`** bình thường
- `mss.grab()` chụp được bình thường
- `SendInput()` hoạt động bình thường → chuột/phím KHÔNG bị treo

---

## Sửa lỗi

Chỉ cần sửa giá trị từ `1` thành `0`:

[Action: replace_file_content]
  AllowMultiple: False
  Description: The docstring says "disable UAC secure desktop switching (PromptOnSecureDesktop = 0)" but the code was setting it to 1 (enabled). This caused UAC prompts to appear on the Secure Desktop, which blocks ...
  EndLine: 183
  Instruction: Fix PromptOnSecureDesktop value from 1 to 0 to actually disable UAC Secure Desktop as intended by the docstring
  ReplacementContent:         winreg.SetValueEx(key, "PromptOnSecureDesktop", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 0)
        winreg.SetValueEx(key, "SoftwareSASGeneration", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 3)
        winreg.CloseKey(key)
        log...

========== MODEL ==========
## Tóm tắt

**Bug**: Ở [windows_service_loop.py dòng 180](file:///d:/Data/AG/remote_desktop/windows_service_loop.py#L180), `PromptOnSecureDesktop` bị đặt = `1` (bật Secure Desktop) thay vì `0` (tắt Secure Desktop). Docstring ghi rõ mục đích tắt, nhưng giá trị bị ngược.

**Hậu quả**: Khi UAC xuất hiện trên máy host Windows 11:
- Hộp thoại UAC hiện trên **Secure Desktop** — một desktop riêng biệt mà process thường không thể tương tác
- **Screen capture** (`mss.grab()`) không chụp được → client thấy màn hình treo
- **Input** (`SendInput()`) không gửi được tới Secure Desktop → chuột/phím bị treo

**Sửa**: Đổi giá trị từ `1` → `0`. Sau khi sửa, UAC prompt sẽ hiện trên desktop `"default"` bình thường, và remote desktop có thể capture + điều khiển bình thường.

> **Lưu ý**: Sau khi deploy bản mới, cần **restart service** trên máy host để registry được cập nhật lại. Hoặc có thể chạy lệnh này trên máy host bị lỗi để sửa tạm ngay:
> ```powershell
> reg add "HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\System" /v PromptOnSecureDesktop /t REG_DWORD /d 0 /f
> ```

========== MODEL ==========
## Tình trạng

Registry `PromptOnSecureDesktop` **vẫn = 1** (giá trị cũ do service cũ đặt). Bản build mới chưa deploy thành vì service SYSTEM đang lock file.

### Bạn cần làm:

**Right-click → Run as administrator** file này:

📄 [deploy_admin.bat](file:///d:/Data/AG/remote_desktop/scratch/deploy_admin.bat)

Script sẽ:
1. Set `PromptOnSecureDesktop = 0` trong registry
2. Kill hết service + agent (với quyền admin)
3. Copy bản build mới vào `C:\Apps\P2P`
4. Restart service
5. Launch app

Sau khi chạy xong, thử lại mở app cần admin trên host — UAC sẽ hiện trên desktop bình thường thay vì Secure Desktop, và remote desktop sẽ không bị treo nữa.

========== USER ==========
tôi đã chạy EasyRemoteDesktop_Installer.exe với quyền admin rồi sao lại còn phải chạy cái này? rồi user sao mà làm được? với lại cái này ở phía host chứ bên máy tôi có liên quan gì đâu

86: def is_secure_desktop():
87:     """Kiểm tra xem thread hiện tại có đang ở Secure Desktop (UAC/Winlogon) không.
88:     Trả về True nếu đang ở Secure Desktop và KHÔNG thể chụp màn hình bình thường."""
89:     try:
90:         # Mở Input Desktop với quyền đọc tối thiểu (DESKTOP_READOBJECTS = 0x0001)
91:         hdesk = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, 0x0001)
92:         if not hdesk:
93:             # Không mở được Input Desktop → đang bị Secure Desktop lock
94:             return True
95:         name = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
96:         ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(hdesk, 2, name, ctypes.sizeof(name), None)
97:         ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(hdesk)
98:         desktop_name = name.value.lower()
99:         # "default" là desktop bình thường; bất kỳ tên nào khác (vd: "winlogon", "secure") là Secure Desktop
100:         return desktop_name not in ("default", "")
101:     except:
102:         return False
103: 
104: def check_desktop_change():
105:     """
106:     Checks the status of active input desktop relative to the current thread.
107:     Returns (needs_switch, is_blocked)
108:     - needs_switch: True if the active input desktop is different from the current thread desktop,
109:                     and we CAN access/switch to it.
110:     - is_blocked: True if the active input desktop cannot be accessed (e.g. secure desktop for normal user).
111:     """
112:     try:
113:         import ctypes
114:         # Try multiple access levels to open the Input Desktop.
115:         # SYSTEM processes (headless agent) can access Secure Desktop with higher rights.
116:         # On Windows 11, when an elevated (Administrator) window has focus, OpenInputDesktop fails
117:         # with Access Denied (5) if requesting high access rights (GENERIC_ALL/GENERIC_READ/READOBJECTS).
118:         # We include DESKTOP_ENUMERATE (0x0040) and zero-mask (0) which succeed to let us read the desktop name.
119:         h_input = None
120:         for access_mask in [0x02000000, 0x80000000, 0x0001, 0x0040, 0]:
121:             h_input = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, access_mask)
122:             if h_input:
123:                 break
124:         
125:         if not h_input:
126:             # Truly blocked (e.g. secure desktop is active and we have no rights to open it even with 0 access)
127:             return False, True
128:             
129:         # Get active input desktop name
130:         name_input = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
131:         ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(h_input, 2, name_input, ctypes.sizeof(name_input), None)
132:         ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_input)
133:         input_name = name_input.value.lower()
134:         
135:         # 2. Get current thread desktop name
136:         thread_name = get_desktop_name()
137:             
138:         if input_name != thread_name:
139:             try:
140:                 # We try to open the target desktop with required access to switch to it
141:                 h_target = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenDesktopW(input_name, 0, False, 0x02000000)
142:                 if h_target:
143:                     ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_target)
144:                     return True, False
145:             except:
146:                 pass
147:             return False, True
148:             
149:         return False, False
150:     except Exception as e:
151:         print(f"[DesktopCheck] Error: {e}")
152:         return False, False
153: 
154: def is_machine_domain_joined():
155:     debug_messages = []

756:     # Host Sender Thread
757:     def host_sender_thread(self, conn, monitor, client_state, password):
758:         print("[Host] Started Screen Sender Thread.")
759:         import io
760:         
761:         try:
762:             conn.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_SNDTIMEO, 2000)
763:         except: pass
764: 
765:         _last_switching_signal_time = 0
766:         while client_state.get("running", False):
767:             try:
768:                 # Early check for desktop status
769:                 needs_switch, is_blocked = check_desktop_change()
770:                 if is_blocked:
771:                     # Throttle: only send switching_desktop signal once every 12 seconds
772:                     # to avoid resetting the client's countdown timer in an infinite loop
773:                     now = time.time()
774:                     if now - _last_switching_signal_time >= 12:
775:                         print("[Host] Secure Desktop detected and cannot be accessed. Signaling client...")
776:                         try:
777:                             signal = json.dumps({"type": "switching_desktop"}).encode('utf-8')
778:                             send_msg(conn, signal, password)
779:                         except:
780:                             pass
781:                         _last_switching_signal_time = now
782:                     time.sleep(0.5)
783:                     continue
784: 
785:                 # Switch thread to active Input Desktop if needed
786:                 if needs_switch:
787:                     _last_switching_signal_time = 0  # Reset throttle so next block event signals immediately
788:                     print("[Host] Desktop change detected. Switching thread desktop...")
789:                     try:
790:                         hdesk = None
791:                         for access_mask in [0x01FF, 0x02000000, 0x80000000, 0x0001, 0]:
792:                             try:
793:                                 hdesk = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, access_mask)
794:                                 if hdesk:
795:                                     break
796:                             except:
797:                                 pass
798:                                 
799:                         if hdesk:
800:                             result = ctypes.windll.user32.SetThreadDesktop(hdesk)
801:                             ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(hdesk)
802:                             if not result:
803:                                 print("[Host] SetThreadDesktop() failed (thread may have existing windows). Retrying...")
804:                                 time.sleep(0.3)
805:                                 continue
806:                         else:
807:                             print("[Host] OpenInputDesktop failed for all access masks.")
808:                             time.sleep(0.3)

========== MODEL ==========
Bây giờ tôi hiểu toàn bộ flow rồi. Vấn đề thực sự có **2 lớp**:

1. **`PromptOnSecureDesktop = 1`** (đã fix trong service) → nhưng nếu service cũ vẫn chạy hoặc Group Policy override, registry vẫn = 1
2. **`check_desktop_change()` quá dễ trả về `is_blocked=True`** → khi `OpenDesktopW(input_name, ..., GENERIC_ALL)` fail, nó "bỏ cuộc" ngay, không thử access mask thấp hơn. Dù agent chạy SYSTEM, trên Win 11 GENERIC_ALL có thể bị chặn.

Để tôi fix cả hai vấn đề:

104: def check_desktop_change():
105:     """
106:     Checks the status of active input desktop relative to the current thread.
107:     Returns (needs_switch, is_blocked)
108:     - needs_switch: True if the active input desktop is different from the current thread desktop,
109:                     and we CAN access/switch to it.
110:     - is_blocked: True if the active input desktop cannot be accessed (e.g. secure desktop for normal user).
111:     """
112:     try:
113:         import ctypes
114:         # Try multiple access levels to open the Input Desktop.
115:         # SYSTEM processes (headless agent) can access Secure Desktop with higher rights.
116:         # On Windows 11, when an elevated (Administrator) window has focus, OpenInputDesktop fails
117:         # with Access Denied (5) if requesting high access rights (GENERIC_ALL/GENERIC_READ/READOBJECTS).
118:         # We include DESKTOP_ENUMERATE (0x0040) and zero-mask (0) which succeed to let us read the desktop name.
119:         h_input = None
120:         for access_mask in [0x02000000, 0x80000000, 0x0001, 0x0040, 0]:
121:             h_input = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenInputDesktop(0, False, access_mask)
122:             if h_input:
123:                 break
124:         
125:         if not h_input:
126:             # Truly blocked (e.g. secure desktop is active and we have no rights to open it even with 0 access)
127:             return False, True
128:             
129:         # Get active input desktop name
130:         name_input = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
131:         ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserObjectInformationW(h_input, 2, name_input, ctypes.sizeof(name_input), None)
132:         ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_input)
133:         input_name = name_input.value.lower()
134:         
135:         # 2. Get current thread desktop name
136:         thread_name = get_desktop_name()
137:             
138:         if input_name != thread_name:
139:             try:
140:                 # We try to open the target desktop with required access to switch to it
141:                 h_target = ctypes.windll.user32.OpenDesktopW(input_name, 0, False, 0x02000000)
142:                 if h_target:
143:                     ctypes.windll.user32.CloseDesktop(h_target)
144:                     return True, False
145:             except:
146:                 pass
147:             return False, True
148:             
149:         return False, False
150:     except Exception as e:
151:         print(f"[DesktopCheck] Error: {e}")
152:         return False, False
153:

========== MODEL ==========
Tôi thấy rõ bug rồi! Dòng 141: `OpenDesktopW(input_name, 0, False, 0x02000000)` - chỉ thử **GENERIC_ALL** duy nhất. Trên Win 11 GENERIC_ALL thường bị Access Denied ngay cả cho SYSTEM. Khi fail → return `is_blocked=True` → agent "bỏ cuộc" → client hiện thông báo "Màn hình bảo mật...".

========== MODEL ==========
Build đang chạy. Tóm tắt các fix đã thực hiện:

## Các thay đổi

### 1. [windows_service_loop.py](file:///d:/Data/AG/remote_desktop/windows_service_loop.py#L180) — Fix registry bug
- `PromptOnSecureDesktop = 1` → `0` (UAC dialog hiện trên desktop bình thường)

### 2. [core/host.py](file:///d:/Data/AG/remote_desktop/core/host.py#L104) — Fix `check_desktop_change()` 
**Root cause chính**: Khi `OpenInputDesktop` thất bại (trên Win11 rất hay xảy ra), hàm này chỉ thử `OpenDesktopW` với **GENERIC_ALL** (0x02000000) duy nhất. Win11 block quyền này ngay cả cho SYSTEM → trả về `is_blocked=True` → agent "bỏ cuộc" → client hiện "Màn hình bảo mật..."

**Fix**: 
- Thử **nhiều access mask** giảm dần khi mở desktop (0x01FF → 0x02000000 → 0x80000000 → 0x0001 → 0x0040 → 0)
- Khi `OpenInputDesktop` thất bại hoàn toàn, **fallback mở trực tiếp "Winlogon" desktop theo tên** (process SYSTEM luôn có quyền)
- Trả về `needs_switch=True` thay vì `is_blocked=True` → agent chuyển desktop và tiếp tục capture

### 3. Sender thread & Receiver thread — Cùng pattern fallback
- Thêm fallback `OpenDesktopW` theo tên khi `OpenInputDesktop` fail

Build xong sẽ tự tạo file `EasyRemoteDesktop_Installer.exe` mới để cài lên host.

160:     'keypad +': 0x6B,
161:     'keypad enter': 0x0D,
162: }
163: 
164: # Track remote modifier key state (set of held modifier key names) to avoid
165: # polling HOST keyboard state via GetAsyncKeyState which is incorrect for remote input.
166: _remote_modifier_keys = set()
167: _remote_modifier_names = {
168:     'left ctrl', 'right ctrl', 'left alt', 'right alt',
169:     'left meta', 'right meta', 'left windows', 'right windows',
170:     'left super', 'right super'
171: }
172: 
173: def send_input_keyboard_event(key_name, pressed):
174:     try:
175:         # Update remote modifier state tracker
176:         if key_name in _remote_modifier_names:
177:             if pressed:
178:                 _remote_modifier_keys.add(key_name)
179:             else:
180:                 _remote_modifier_keys.discard(key_name)
181: 
182:         vk = None
183:         if key_name in vk_map:
184:             vk = vk_map[key_name]
185:         elif len(key_name) == 1:
186:             char_upper = key_name.upper()
187:             if ('A' <= char_upper <= 'Z') or ('0' <= char_upper <= '9'):
188:                 vk = ord(char_upper)
189:             else:
190:                 # Try to resolve VK code for punctuation/symbol characters via VkKeyScanW
191:                 # This enables Vietnamese IME (Unikey) to process keys like [, ], ;, ', etc.
192:                 vk_scan = ctypes.windll.user32.VkKeyScanW(ord(key_name))
193:                 if vk_scan != -1 and (vk_scan & 0xFF) != 0:
194:                     vk = vk_scan & 0xFF  # Low byte = VK code
195:                 
196:         # Check if any shortcut modifier is held down based on tracked remote state
197:         is_modifier = bool(_remote_modifier_keys)
198:         
199:         if vk is not None:
200:             # Use VK code + hardware scan code for all keys that have a VK mapping.
201:             # This is critical for Vietnamese IME (Unikey/Telex/VNI) compatibility:
202:             # Unikey hooks WM_KEYDOWN/WM_KEYUP messages and needs real VK codes
203:             # to detect key sequences (e.g. a+a -> â, o+w -> ơ).
204:             # KEYEVENTF_UNICODE bypasses keyboard hooks so Unikey cannot intercept it.
205:             inp = INPUT()
206:             inp.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD
207:             flags = 0
208:             if not pressed:
209:                 flags |= KEYEVENTF_KEYUP
210:                 
211:             # Get hardware scan code from VK for maximum compatibility
212:             scan = ctypes.windll.user32.MapVirtualKeyW(vk, 0)  # MAPVK_VK_TO_VSC
213:                 
214:             # Check for extended keys
215:             extended_vks = [
216:                 0x25, 0x26, 0x27, 0x28, # Arrows
217:                 0x2D, 0x2E,             # Insert, Delete
218:                 0x24, 0x23,             # Home, End
219:                 0x21, 0x22,             # PageUp, PageDown
220:                 0x90,                   # Numlock
221:                 0x2F,                   # Print screen
222:                 0xA5,                   # VK_RMENU (Right Alt)
223:                 0xA3,                   # VK_RCONTROL (Right Ctrl)
224:                 0x5B, 0x5C,             # LWIN, RWIN
225:                 0x5D                    # VK_APPS (Menu/Application key)
226:             ]
227:             if vk in extended_vks:
228:                 flags |= KEYEVENTF_EXTENDEDKEY
229:                 
230:             # Map left/right modifiers to their generic VK equivalents.
231:             # This is critical for the Windows Login Screen (Secure Desktop),
232:             # which often ignores directional modifiers (like VK_LSHIFT = 0xA0)
233:             # when injected via SendInput.
234:             generic_vks = {
235:                 0xA0: 0x10, # VK_LSHIFT -> VK_SHIFT
236:                 0xA1: 0x10, # VK_RSHIFT -> VK_SHIFT
237:                 0xA2: 0x11, # VK_LCONTROL -> VK_CONTROL
238:                 0xA3: 0x11, # VK_RCONTROL -> VK_CONTROL
239:                 0xA4: 0x12, # VK_LMENU -> VK_MENU
240:                 0xA5: 0x12, # VK_RMENU -> VK_MENU
241:             }
242:             inject_vk = generic_vks.get(vk, vk)
243:                 
244:             inp.union.ki = KEYBDINPUT(inject_vk, scan, flags, 0, None)
245:             ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
246:         elif len(key_name) == 1 and not is_modifier:
247:             # Fallback: Use KEYEVENTF_UNICODE only for characters without a VK code
248:             # (e.g. pre-composed Unicode characters, emoji, special symbols)
249:             inp = INPUT()
250:             inp.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD
251:             flags = KEYEVENTF_UNICODE
252:             if not pressed:
253:                 flags |= KEYEVENTF_KEYUP
254:             inp.union.ki = KEYBDINPUT(0, ord(key_name), flags, 0, None)
255:             ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
256:     except Exception as e:
257:         print(f"[SendInput] Keyboard injection failed: {e}")
258: 
259: def send_input_mouse_click(button_name, pressed):
260:     try:
261:         flags = 0
262:         if button_name == 'left':
263:             flags = MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN if pressed else MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP
264:         elif button_name == 'right':
265:             flags = MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN if pressed else MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP
266:         elif button_name == 'middle':
267:             flags = MOUSEEVENTF_MIDDLEDOWN if pressed else MOUSEEVENTF_MIDDLEUP
268:             
269:         if flags:
270:             inp = INPUT()
271:             inp.type = INPUT_MOUSE
272:             inp.union.mi = MOUSEINPUT(0, 0, 0, flags, 0, None)
273:             ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
274:     except Exception as e:
275:         print(f"[SendInput] Mouse click injection failed: {e}")
276: 
277: def send_input_mouse_scroll(dx, dy):
278:     try:
279:         if dy != 0:
280:             inp = INPUT()
281:             inp.type = INPUT_MOUSE
282:             inp.union.mi = MOUSEINPUT(0, 0, int(dy * 120), MOUSEEVENTF_WHEEL, 0, None)
283:             ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
284:     except Exception as e:
285:         print(f"[SendInput] Mouse scroll injection failed: {e}")
286: 
287: # Cached screen metrics for mouse move normalization (avoid calling GetSystemMetrics on every event)
288: _cached_screen_w = 0
289: _cached_screen_h = 0
290: _cached_screen_time = 0
291: 
292: def send_input_mouse_move(x, y):
293:     global _cached_screen_w, _cached_screen_h, _cached_screen_time
294:     try:
295:         # Refresh cached screen dimensions every 2 seconds
296:         now = time.monotonic() if hasattr(time, 'monotonic') else time.time()
297:         if now - _cached_screen_time > 2.0 or _cached_screen_w == 0:
298:             _cached_screen_w = ctypes.windll.user32.GetSystemMetrics(0) # SM_CXSCREEN
299:             _cached_screen_h = ctypes.windll.user32.GetSystemMetrics(1) # SM_CYSCREEN
300:             _cached_screen_time = now
301:         w, h = _cached_screen_w, _cached_screen_h
302:         if w > 0 and h > 0:
303:             # Standard absolute coordinate formula: (coord * 65535) / (screen_size - 1)
304:             normalized_x = int((x * 65535) / (w - 1)) if w > 1 else 0
305:             normalized_y = int((y * 65535) / (h - 1)) if h > 1 else 0
306:             inp = INPUT()
307:             inp.type = INPUT_MOUSE
308:             # MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE = 0x0001, MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE = 0x8000
309:             inp.union.mi = MOUSEINPUT(normalized_x, normalized_y, 0, 0x0001 | 0x8000, 0, None)
310:             ctypes.windll.user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(INPUT))
311:     except Exception as e:
312:         print(f"[SendInput] Mouse move injection failed: {e}")

67: _orig_toplevel_geometry = tk.Toplevel.geometry
68: def _scaled_toplevel_geometry(self, newGeometry=None):
69:     if newGeometry is None:
70:         return _orig_toplevel_geometry(self)
71:     try:
72:         import re
73:         m = re.match(r'^(\d+)x(\d+)(?:\+([+-]?\d+)\+([+-]?\d+))?$', newGeometry)
74:         if m:
75:             w = int(m.group(1))
76:             h = int(m.group(2))
77:             x = m.group(3)
78:             y = m.group(4)
79:             scale = self.winfo_fpixels('1i') / 96.0
80:             sw = int(w * scale)
81:             sh = int(h * scale)
82:             if x is not None and y is not None:
83:                 adj_x = int(x) - (sw - w) // 2
84:                 adj_y = int(y) - (sh - h) // 2
85:                 newGeometry = f"{sw}x{sh}+{adj_x}+{adj_y}"
86:             else:
87:                 newGeometry = f"{sw}x{sh}"
88:     except Exception as e:
89:         pass
90:     return _orig_toplevel_geometry(self, newGeometry)

45:         if sys.platform != "win32":
46:             return
47:         try:
48:             import winreg
49:             path = r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\System"

61:     def wake_on_lan(self, mac_str):
62:         # mac_str can be multiple MACs separated by comma
63:         for m in mac_str.split(','):
64:             m = m.strip()
65:             if not m: continue
66:             try:
67:                 # Remove common separators
68:                 mac = m.replace(':', '').replace('-', '').replace('.', '')
69:                 if len(mac) != 12:
70:                     continue
71:                 data = bytes.fromhex('F' * 12 + mac * 16)
72:                 sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
73:                 sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_BROADCAST, 1)
74:                 try:
75:                     sock.sendto(data, ('255.255.255.255', 9))
76:                 except:
77:                     pass
78:                 # Try subnet broadcasts
79:                 try:
80:                     local_ips = getattr(self, 'local_ip', get_local_ip()).split(',')
81:                     for lip in local_ips:
82:                         lip = lip.strip()
83:                         if lip and not lip.startswith('127.'):
84:                             parts = lip.split('.')
85:                             if len(parts) == 4:
86:                                 subnet_broadcast = f"{parts[0]}.{parts[1]}.{parts[2]}.255"
87:                                 sock.sendto(data, (subnet_broadcast, 9))
88:                 except:
89:                     pass
90:                 sock.close()
91:                 self.update_status(_("Đã gửi Wake-On-Lan tới MAC {mac}").format(mac=m))
92:             except Exception as e:
93:                 print(f"[WOL] Lỗi gửi Wake-On-Lan tới MAC {m}: {e}")
94: 
95:     # ==================== LAN DISCOVERY (UDP Broadcast) ====================
96:     def start_lan_discovery(self):
97:         """Khởi chạy 2 luồng: beacon broadcaster và beacon listener cho LAN Discovery."""
98:         threading.Thread(target=self._lan_beacon_sender, daemon=True).start()
99:         threading.Thread(target=self._lan_beacon_listener, daemon=True).start()
100:         print("[LAN Discovery] Started beacon sender and listener threads.")
101: 
102:     def _lan_beacon_sender(self):
103:         """Phát UDP broadcast beacon mỗi LAN_BEACON_INTERVAL giây."""
104:         import platform
105:         while getattr(self, 'running_server', True):
106:             if not self.is_headless and getattr(self, "is_service_active", False):
107:                 # Service is active, GUI app should not broadcast beacon to avoid hijacking LAN connection
108:                 time.sleep(LAN_BEACON_INTERVAL)
109:                 continue
110:             try:
111:                 beacon = json.dumps({
112:                     "sig": LAN_APP_SIGNATURE,
113:                     "hwid": self.my_id_clean,

118:                 }).encode('utf-8')
119:                 
120:                 sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
121:                 sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_BROADCAST, 1)
122:                 sock.settimeout(1.0)
123:                 try:
124:                     sock.sendto(beacon, ('255.255.255.255', LAN_DISCOVERY_PORT))
125:                 except Exception:
126:                     pass
127:                 # Gửi thêm tới các subnet broadcast cụ thể (hỗ trợ router chặn global broadcast)
128:                 try:
129:                     local_ips = getattr(self, 'local_ip', '').split(',')
130:                     for lip in local_ips:
131:                         lip = lip.strip()
132:                         if lip and not lip.startswith('127.'):
133:                             parts = lip.split('.')
134:                             if len(parts) == 4:
135:                                 subnet_broadcast = f"{parts[0]}.{parts[1]}.{parts[2]}.255"
136:                                 sock.sendto(beacon, (subnet_broadcast, LAN_DISCOVERY_PORT))
137:                 except Exception:
138:                     pass
139:                 sock.close()
140:             except Exception as e:
141:                 print(f"[LAN Discovery] Beacon send error: {e}")
142:             time.sleep(LAN_BEACON_INTERVAL)

634:     def signaling_maintainer_thread(self, host):
635:         retry_delay = 2  # Bắt đầu retry nhanh (2s), tăng dần sau 3 lần thất bại
636:         fail_count = 0
637:         while self.running_server:
638:             sock = None
639:             try:
640:                 # 1. Connect
641:                 sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
642:                 sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
643:                 try:
644:                     sock.bind(('0.0.0.0', BOUND_PORT))
645:                 except Exception as e:
646:                     print(f"[Signaling] Warning: Could not bind to BOUND_PORT {BOUND_PORT} for signaling: {e}")
647:                     
648:                 try:
649:                     if os.name == 'nt':
650:                         sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_KEEPALIVE, 1)
651:                         sock.ioctl(socket.SIOC_KEEPALIVE_VALS, (1, 30000, 10000))
652:                     else:
653:                         sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_KEEPALIVE, 1)
654:                 except Exception: pass
655:                 
656:                 sock.settimeout(5.0)
657:                 sock.connect((host, core.config.SIGNALING_SERVER_PORT))
658:                 sock.settimeout(None)
659:                 
660:                 # Use base HWID if we are the primary instance (port 12345), else append port to avoid stealing ID from background service
661:                 is_svc_active = False
662:                 if hasattr(self, 'check_if_service_active'):
663:                     is_svc_active = self.check_if_service_active()
664:                 else:
665:                     is_svc_active = getattr(self, 'is_service_active', False)
666:                 register_id = self.my_id_clean if (BOUND_PORT == PORTS_TO_TRY[0] and not is_svc_active) else f"{self.my_id_clean}_{BOUND_PORT}"
667:                 
668:                 req = json.dumps({"action": "register", "hwid": register_id})
669:                 req_data = req.encode('utf-8')
670:                 send_msg(sock, req_data, APP_KEY)
671:                 
672:                 with self.signaling_lock:
673:                     self.signaling_sockets[host] = sock
674:                     if getattr(self, 'current_signaling_host', None) is None:
675:                         self.current_signaling_host = host
676:                         self.primary_signaling_socket = sock

========== MODEL ==========
Tôi đã tìm ra nguyên nhân vì sao UAC hiện lên ở phía viewer nhưng bạn không thể bấm được nút **Yes** hay **No**:

### Nguyên nhân
Dù trước đó tôi đã sửa cấu hình Registry `PromptOnSecureDesktop = 0` ở tiến trình service (`windows_service_loop.py`), nhưng trong code của **Agent chính** (`app.py` và `core/network_manager.py`) vẫn còn hàm cấu hình ghi đè giá trị này về `1` (`PromptOnSecureDesktop = 1`) mỗi khi Agent khởi động hoặc kết nối mạng.

* Do đó, Registry của Windows luôn bị ép về `1` (bật Secure Desktop).
* Khi Secure Desktop bật, UAC sẽ chạy trên desktop bảo mật độc lập (`winlogon`). Mặc dù tiến trình Agent ở quyền `SYSTEM` có thể nhảy sang desktop này để chụp màn hình gửi về cho bạn (nên bạn vẫn nhìn thấy hộp thoại), nhưng Windows **chặn tuyệt đối việc giả lập nút bấm** chuột/phím từ xa khi ở trên Secure Desktop.
* Khi Registry được đưa về `0`, hộp thoại UAC sẽ chạy trực tiếp trên desktop thường (`default`). Khi đó, Agent chạy quyền `SYSTEM` sẽ giả lập click chuột vào nút **Yes**/**No** bình thường mà không bị Windows chặn nữa.

---

### Giải pháp
Tôi đã tìm tất cả các file cấu hình Registry và sửa giá trị `PromptOnSecureDesktop` thành `0` đồng bộ trên toàn hệ thống. 

Ứng dụng đang được tự động build lại (sử dụng Nuitka). Khi quá trình build kết thúc, file cài đặt `EasyRemoteDesktop_Installer.exe` mới sẽ sẵn sàng. Bạn chỉ cần chạy lại installer trên máy Host để cập nhật.
